====================================================================================================
QUESTION: What happens if either insured commits suicide within 2 years of the issue date?
CATEGORY: CAT3_CONDITIONAL_REASONING
EXPECTED ANSWER: The death benefit is limited to return of premiums paid. This suicide clause resets if the policy is reinstated.
EVIDENCE: 7.1 SUICIDE CLAUSE: If either insured commits suicide within 2 years of the issue date, the death benefit is limited to return of premiums paid. / 7.2 This suicide clause resets if the policy is reinstated.
====================================================================================================

--- GOAL PLAN ---
rewritten_query: What is the consequence if an insured person dies by suicide within two years of the policy's issue date?
reasoning: This query is asking about a specific clause in an insurance policy, particularly related to life insurance. The key elements are: "insured" (a person covered by the policy), "suicide", "within 2 years of the issue date", and the consequence of that event. This is a well-known provision in life insurance policies: most policies include a suicide clause that excludes payout if the insured dies by suicide within the first two years of the policy's issuance. The answer is a standard insurance industry rule, not requiring multiple hops through unrelated facts. The query is focused on a single, specific policy condition, so only one sub-goal is needed.
